- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/axi_90x90_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** hypex with an OS throat blend over [0, throat_blend_mm], 0-30 mm (4 knobs)

#### `L_mm` — Depth (mm)

- **Summary:** Axial depth shared by both meridians, throat to mouth.
- **Detail:** The generator builds both meridians to one z span, so L_mm is shared rather than a per-plane length. It is the fixed-length dimension for conical, exponential, hypex and OSSE; length-derived types may only be shortened to it. The brief envelope still bounds the final depth.
- **Formula:** `z = s L, with s the normalised axial fraction`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the mouth plane in or out along the horn axis.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 190.0 (source: `examples/typologies/throatblend_hypex.yaml` `seed_params.L_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 120.0 .. 220.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/throatblend_hypex.yaml` `bounds.L_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.L_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ## 4. FREEFORM
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_T` — H-plane hypex T factor

- **Summary:** Selects the hyperbolic-exponential member between catenoidal and exponential.
- **Detail:** Hypex reads T directly in its cosh/sinh wall law. The mesher-side T is a shape factor only; the generator solves the flare constant so the wall reaches the requested mouth radius.
- **Formula:** `r = r0 (cosh(m z) + T sinh(m z))`
- **Physical effect:** Changes how quickly the wall leaves the throat for a fixed mouth.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.7 (source: `examples/typologies/throatblend_hypex.yaml` `seed_params.h_T`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/throatblend_hypex.yaml` `bounds.h_T`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.hypex`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ### 13.3 Salmon lineage corrections + the X reading
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `h_rm_mm` — H-plane mouth radius (mm)

- **Summary:** Per-plane target mouth radius for conical, exponential and hypex walls.
- **Detail:** For the fixed-size classical walls this is the radius the meridian must reach at its end; the flare is solved from the throat radius to it. It is per meridian: the H and V planes carry their own value. The QTW arc rule derives its mouth radius instead of taking this knob.
- **Formula:** `conical: r(z) = r0 + z tan(alpha); exponential: r = r0 exp(m z)`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the mouth area and therefore the low-frequency loading for these walls.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 140.0 (source: `examples/typologies/throatblend_hypex.yaml` `seed_params.h_rm_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 60.0 .. 175.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/throatblend_hypex.yaml` `bounds.h_rm_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ### 16.1 The typology map, dated and sourced
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_throat_blend_mm` — H-plane throat blend length (mm)

- **Summary:** Length over which an OS throat is blended into a classical wall; zero skips it.
- **Detail:** A nonzero length replaces the start of the classical meridian with an OS throat that starts at the driver exit angle. A smoothstep blend lands exactly on the classical radius and angle at the end of the blend. The default zero path leaves the parent bit-for-bit unchanged.
- **Formula:** `r(z) = (1 - w) r_OS(z) + w r_classical(z), w = 3t^2 - 2t^3, t = z / L_b`
- **Physical effect:** Trades a short OS throat transition against the parent's own throat shape.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 8.0 (source: `examples/typologies/throatblend_hypex.yaml` `seed_params.h_throat_blend_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 30.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/throatblend_hypex.yaml` `bounds.h_throat_blend_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.throat_blend`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.2 `throat_blend_mm` (G3)
- **Flags:** —

#### `max_wall_deg` — Generator wall-angle cap (deg)

- **Summary:** Upper bound applied to every selected anchor's wall angle.
- **Detail:** The materializer clamps anchor angles into this cap after selecting and perturbing them. The same block key also supplies the default stop for a spherical-salmon march.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Caps how vertical the wall may become.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 80.0 (source: `examples/typologies/throatblend_hypex.yaml` `generator.max_wall_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.3 `spherical_salmon` (G4)

## Group: hybrid

### blend_conical_exponential — FREEFORM / generator

- **Group:** hybrid
- **Symmetry:** axisymmetric
- **Config:** `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml`
- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/axi_90x90_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** convex blend of conical and exponential at the same size, axisymmetric

#### `L_mm` — Depth (mm)

- **Summary:** Axial depth shared by both meridians, throat to mouth.
- **Detail:** The generator builds both meridians to one z span, so L_mm is shared rather than a per-plane length. It is the fixed-length dimension for conical, exponential, hypex and OSSE; length-derived types may only be shortened to it. The brief envelope still bounds the final depth.
- **Formula:** `z = s L, with s the normalised axial fraction`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the mouth plane in or out along the horn axis.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 180.0 (source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `seed_params.L_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 120.0 .. 220.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `bounds.L_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.L_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ## 4. FREEFORM
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_rm_mm` — H-plane mouth radius (mm)

- **Summary:** Per-plane target mouth radius for conical, exponential and hypex walls.
- **Detail:** For the fixed-size classical walls this is the radius the meridian must reach at its end; the flare is solved from the throat radius to it. It is per meridian: the H and V planes carry their own value. The QTW arc rule derives its mouth radius instead of taking this knob.
- **Formula:** `conical: r(z) = r0 + z tan(alpha); exponential: r = r0 exp(m z)`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the mouth area and therefore the low-frequency loading for these walls.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 130.0 (source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `seed_params.h_rm_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 60.0 .. 175.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `bounds.h_rm_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ### 16.1 The typology map, dated and sourced
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_w` — H-plane blend weight

- **Summary:** Convex weight between the primary meridian and its second (blend) parent.
- **Detail:** The blend parent is built from the same throat and depth, then mixed with the primary wall at normalised axial position. A weight of zero returns the primary wall and a weight of one returns the second parent.
- **Formula:** `r(t) = (1 - w) r_a(t) + w r_b(t)`
- **Physical effect:** Slides the composite wall between the two parent shapes.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.5 (source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `seed_params.h_w`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `bounds.h_w`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.blend`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ### 16.1 The typology map, dated and sourced
- **Flags:** —

#### `hb_rm_mm` — H-plane mouth radius (blend parent) (mm)

- **Summary:** Per-plane target mouth radius for conical, exponential and hypex walls.
- **Detail:** For the fixed-size classical walls this is the radius the meridian must reach at its end; the flare is solved from the throat radius to it. It is per meridian: the H and V planes carry their own value. The QTW arc rule derives its mouth radius instead of taking this knob.
- **Formula:** `conical: r(z) = r0 + z tan(alpha); exponential: r = r0 exp(m z)`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the mouth area and therefore the low-frequency loading for these walls.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 130.0 (source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `seed_params.hb_rm_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 60.0 .. 175.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `bounds.hb_rm_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ### 16.1 The typology map, dated and sourced
- **Flags:** —

#### `max_wall_deg` — Generator wall-angle cap (deg)

- **Summary:** Upper bound applied to every selected anchor's wall angle.
- **Detail:** The materializer clamps anchor angles into this cap after selecting and perturbing them. The same block key also supplies the default stop for a spherical-salmon march.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Caps how vertical the wall may become.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 80.0 (source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `generator.max_wall_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.3 `spherical_salmon` (G4)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `mouth` — Mouth cross-section

- **Summary:** Mouth cross-section family: ellipse, superellipse or rounded rectangle.
- **Detail:** The generator emits two cross-section stations: a circular throat and the selected mouth shape. Superellipse and rounded-rectangle mouths read the mouth exponent and corner-radius knobs respectively.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Changes the mouth outline without changing the meridians.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** ellipse (source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `generator.mouth`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4d. Cross-section stations (crossSections)
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_anchors` — Anchor count

- **Summary:** Number of [z, r, wall-angle] anchors selected per meridian.
- **Detail:** The generator reduces the dense march to this many anchors before the mesher sees it. Both endpoints are always kept and interior anchors are placed by equal arc length in the normalised z/angle plane, so curved regions get more anchors.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises mesh fidelity at the cost of more control points.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 16 (source: `examples/typologies/blend_conical_exponential.yaml` `generator.n_anchors`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.select_anchors`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4b. Profile representation
- **Flags:** —

### blend_osse_tractrix — FREEFORM / generator

- **Group:** hybrid
- **Symmetry:** axisymmetric
- **Config:** `examples/typologies/blend_osse_tractrix.yaml`
- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/axi_90x90_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** convex blend (1-w) OSSE + w tractrix, axisymmetric

#### `L_mm` — Depth (mm)

- **Summary:** Axial depth shared by both meridians, throat to mouth.
- **Detail:** The generator builds both meridians to one z span, so L_mm is shared rather than a per-plane length. It is the fixed-length dimension for conical, exponential, hypex and OSSE; length-derived types may only be shortened to it. The brief envelope still bounds the final depth.
- **Formula:** `z = s L, with s the normalised axial fraction`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the mouth plane in or out along the horn axis.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 120.0 (source: `examples/typologies/blend_osse_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.L_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 90.0 .. 170.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/blend_osse_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.L_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.L_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ## 4. FREEFORM
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_a_deg` — H-plane OSSE coverage half-angle (deg)

- **Summary:** Far-field wall half-angle of the OSSE meridian; the throat angle is the driver exit.
- **Detail:** The OSSE wall starts at the brief's driver exit angle and approaches this coverage half-angle. It is a half-angle in the mesher's convention.
- **Formula:** `OSSE: a is the far-field wall half-angle`
- **Physical effect:** Widens or narrows the OSSE mouth for a fixed depth.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 45.0 (source: `examples/typologies/blend_osse_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.h_a_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 30.0 .. 55.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/blend_osse_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.h_a_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.a_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `h_w` — H-plane blend weight

- **Summary:** Convex weight between the primary meridian and its second (blend) parent.
- **Detail:** The blend parent is built from the same throat and depth, then mixed with the primary wall at normalised axial position. A weight of zero returns the primary wall and a weight of one returns the second parent.
- **Formula:** `r(t) = (1 - w) r_a(t) + w r_b(t)`
- **Physical effect:** Slides the composite wall between the two parent shapes.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.5 (source: `examples/typologies/blend_osse_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.h_w`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/blend_osse_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.h_w`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.blend`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ### 16.1 The typology map, dated and sourced
- **Flags:** —

#### `hb_fc_hz` — H-plane cutoff frequency (blend parent) (Hz)

- **Summary:** Plane-wave cutoff that fixes the flare constant or the tractrix mouth size.
- **Detail:** Tractrix and the Le Cleac'h family size their mouth from this cutoff; the exponential family derives its flare constant from it. It is a frequency, not a wall angle, and it does not change the brief's analysis band.
- **Formula:** `m = 4 pi fc / c (Le Cleac'h); a = c / (2 pi fc) (tractrix mouth radius)`
- **Physical effect:** Raises or lowers the corner around which the horn stops loading.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 700.0 (source: `examples/typologies/blend_osse_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.hb_fc_hz`)
- **Search bounds:** 450.0 .. 1200.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/blend_osse_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.hb_fc_hz`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1a. Mathematical formula
- **Flags:** —

#### `max_wall_deg` — Generator wall-angle cap (deg)

- **Summary:** Upper bound applied to every selected anchor's wall angle.

- **Physical effect:** Caps how vertical the wall may become.

#### `h_s` — H-plane termination amplitude

- **Summary:** OSSE superellipse termination amplitude; zero disables the termination.
- **Detail:** A nonzero s turns on the OSSE superellipse termination and makes n and q active. The mesher requires a positive n and q while s is nonzero. A zero value leaves the plain OS wall.
- **Formula:** `OSSE superellipse termination (pkg/profile_formulas.py)`
- **Physical effect:** Rolls the mouth toward a superellipse outline instead of a plain OS wall.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.3 (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_hypex_v.yaml` `seed_params.h_s`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 0.6 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_hypex_v.yaml` `bounds.h_s`)

- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry._osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters

- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 16 (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_hypex_v.yaml` `generator.n_anchors`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.select_anchors`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4b. Profile representation
- **Flags:** —

#### `v_T` — V-plane hypex T factor

- **Summary:** Selects the hyperbolic-exponential member between catenoidal and exponential.
- **Detail:** Hypex reads T directly in its cosh/sinh wall law. The mesher-side T is a shape factor only; the generator solves the flare constant so the wall reaches the requested mouth radius.
- **Formula:** `r = r0 (cosh(m z) + T sinh(m z))`
- **Physical effect:** Changes how quickly the wall leaves the throat for a fixed mouth.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.7 (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_hypex_v.yaml` `seed_params.v_T`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_hypex_v.yaml` `bounds.v_T`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.hypex`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ### 13.3 Salmon lineage corrections + the X reading
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `v_rm_mm` — V-plane mouth radius (mm)

- **Summary:** Per-plane target mouth radius for conical, exponential and hypex walls.
- **Detail:** For the fixed-size classical walls this is the radius the meridian must reach at its end; the flare is solved from the throat radius to it. It is per meridian: the H and V planes carry their own value. The QTW arc rule derives its mouth radius instead of taking this knob.
- **Formula:** `conical: r(z) = r0 + z tan(alpha); exponential: r = r0 exp(m z)`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the mouth area and therefore the low-frequency loading for these walls.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 100.0 (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_hypex_v.yaml` `seed_params.v_rm_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 50.0 .. 140.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_hypex_v.yaml` `bounds.v_rm_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ### 16.1 The typology map, dated and sourced
- **Flags:** —

### hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v — FREEFORM / generator

- **Group:** hybrid
- **Symmetry:** hv
- **Config:** `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml`
- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/hifi_90x60_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** H/V hybrid, OSSE horizontal x tractrix vertical, 75 deg wall cap

#### `L_mm` — Depth (mm)

- **Summary:** Axial depth shared by both meridians, throat to mouth.
- **Detail:** The generator builds both meridians to one z span, so L_mm is shared rather than a per-plane length. It is the fixed-length dimension for conical, exponential, hypex and OSSE; length-derived types may only be shortened to it. The brief envelope still bounds the final depth.
- **Formula:** `z = s L, with s the normalised axial fraction`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the mouth plane in or out along the horn axis.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 120.0 (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `seed_params.L_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 90.0 .. 170.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `bounds.L_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.L_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ## 4. FREEFORM
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_a_deg` — H-plane OSSE coverage half-angle (deg)

- **Summary:** Far-field wall half-angle of the OSSE meridian; the throat angle is the driver exit.
- **Detail:** The OSSE wall starts at the brief's driver exit angle and approaches this coverage half-angle. It is a half-angle in the mesher's convention.
- **Formula:** `OSSE: a is the far-field wall half-angle`
- **Physical effect:** Widens or narrows the OSSE mouth for a fixed depth.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 45.0 (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `seed_params.h_a_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 35.0 .. 55.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `bounds.h_a_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.a_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `h_s` — H-plane termination amplitude

- **Summary:** OSSE superellipse termination amplitude; zero disables the termination.
- **Detail:** A nonzero s turns on the OSSE superellipse termination and makes n and q active. The mesher requires a positive n and q while s is nonzero. A zero value leaves the plain OS wall.
- **Formula:** `OSSE superellipse termination (pkg/profile_formulas.py)`
- **Physical effect:** Rolls the mouth toward a superellipse outline instead of a plain OS wall.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.3 (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `seed_params.h_s`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 0.8 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `bounds.h_s`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.s`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry._osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `max_wall_deg` — Generator wall-angle cap (deg)

- **Summary:** Upper bound applied to every selected anchor's wall angle.
- **Detail:** The materializer clamps anchor angles into this cap after selecting and perturbing them. The same block key also supplies the default stop for a spherical-salmon march.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Caps how vertical the wall may become.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 75.0 (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `generator.max_wall_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.3 `spherical_salmon` (G4)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `mouth` — Mouth cross-section

- **Summary:** Mouth cross-section family: ellipse, superellipse or rounded rectangle.
- **Detail:** The generator emits two cross-section stations: a circular throat and the selected mouth shape. Superellipse and rounded-rectangle mouths read the mouth exponent and corner-radius knobs respectively.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Changes the mouth outline without changing the meridians.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** ellipse (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `generator.mouth`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4d. Cross-section stations (crossSections)
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_anchors` — Anchor count

- **Summary:** Number of [z, r, wall-angle] anchors selected per meridian.
- **Detail:** The generator reduces the dense march to this many anchors before the mesher sees it. Both endpoints are always kept and interior anchors are placed by equal arc length in the normalised z/angle plane, so curved regions get more anchors.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises mesh fidelity at the cost of more control points.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 16 (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `generator.n_anchors`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.select_anchors`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4b. Profile representation
- **Flags:** —

#### `v_fc_hz` — V-plane cutoff frequency (Hz)

- **Summary:** Plane-wave cutoff that fixes the flare constant or the tractrix mouth size.
- **Detail:** Tractrix and the Le Cleac'h family size their mouth from this cutoff; the exponential family derives its flare constant from it. It is a frequency, not a wall angle, and it does not change the brief's analysis band.
- **Formula:** `m = 4 pi fc / c (Le Cleac'h); a = c / (2 pi fc) (tractrix mouth radius)`
- **Physical effect:** Raises or lowers the corner around which the horn stops loading.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 650.0 (source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `seed_params.v_fc_hz`)
- **Search bounds:** 450.0 .. 1000.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/hyb_osse_h_tractrix_v.yaml` `bounds.v_fc_hz`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1a. Mathematical formula
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_u0` — H-plane perturbation strength

- **Summary:** Per-anchor log-increment perturbation applied to the anchor radii.
- **Detail:** Each interior anchor increment is scaled by exp(u) before the radii are rebuilt from the throat. The legacy form moves the mouth size too, while the shape mode rescales the increments to preserve the parent mouth radius. A zero knob leaves the parent anchors untouched.
- **Formula:** `new_r[i+1] = new_r[i] + d_i exp(clip(u_i, -u_max, u_max))`
- **Physical effect:** Redistributes wall expansion between anchors.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.0 (source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_osse_hv.yaml` `seed_params.h_u0`)
- **Search bounds:** -0.25 .. 0.25 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_osse_hv.yaml` `bounds.h_u0`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.perturb_increments`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/senior_pass_2026-10-24/04_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` § ## WP3. Shape-only perturbations and graded infeasibility
- **Flags:** —

#### `perturb.mode` — Perturbation mode

- **Summary:** Legacy moves mouth size with the perturbation; shape preserves the parent radius.
- **Detail:** Legacy applies the exponential increments directly, which changes the mouth as well as the wall shape. Shape rescales the perturbed increments so the sum, and therefore the mouth radius, is unchanged. An absent mode behaves as legacy.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Chooses whether the perturbation also changes the mouth size.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** — (source: —)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)

#### `v_u0` — V-plane perturbation strength

- **Summary:** Per-anchor log-increment perturbation applied to the anchor radii.
- **Detail:** Each interior anchor increment is scaled by exp(u) before the radii are rebuilt from the throat. The legacy form moves the mouth size too, while the shape mode rescales the increments to preserve the parent mouth radius. A zero knob leaves the parent anchors untouched.
- **Formula:** `new_r[i+1] = new_r[i] + d_i exp(clip(u_i, -u_max, u_max))`
- **Physical effect:** Redistributes wall expansion between anchors.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.0 (source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_osse_hv.yaml` `seed_params.v_u0`)
- **Search bounds:** -0.25 .. 0.25 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_osse_hv.yaml` `bounds.v_u0`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.perturb_increments`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/senior_pass_2026-10-24/04_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` § ## WP3. Shape-only perturbations and graded infeasibility

### perturbed_tractrix — FREEFORM / generator

- **Group:** hybrid
- **Symmetry:** axisymmetric
- **Config:** `examples/typologies/perturbed_tractrix.yaml`
- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/axi_90x90_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** tractrix + monotone increment perturbation (8 knobs)

#### `L_mm` — Depth (mm)

- **Summary:** Axial depth shared by both meridians, throat to mouth.
- **Detail:** The generator builds both meridians to one z span, so L_mm is shared rather than a per-plane length. It is the fixed-length dimension for conical, exponential, hypex and OSSE; length-derived types may only be shortened to it. The brief envelope still bounds the final depth.
- **Formula:** `z = s L, with s the normalised axial fraction`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the mouth plane in or out along the horn axis.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 220.0 (source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.L_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.L_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ## 4. FREEFORM
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_fc_hz` — H-plane cutoff frequency (Hz)

- **Summary:** Plane-wave cutoff that fixes the flare constant or the tractrix mouth size.
- **Detail:** Tractrix and the Le Cleac'h family size their mouth from this cutoff; the exponential family derives its flare constant from it. It is a frequency, not a wall angle, and it does not change the brief's analysis band.
- **Formula:** `m = 4 pi fc / c (Le Cleac'h); a = c / (2 pi fc) (tractrix mouth radius)`
- **Physical effect:** Raises or lowers the corner around which the horn stops loading.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 600.0 (source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.h_fc_hz`)
- **Search bounds:** 330.0 .. 1400.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.h_fc_hz`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1a. Mathematical formula
- **Flags:** —

- **Summary:** Per-anchor log-increment perturbation applied to the anchor radii.
- **Detail:** Each interior anchor increment is scaled by exp(u) before the radii are rebuilt from the throat. The legacy form moves the mouth size too, while the shape mode rescales the increments to preserve the parent mouth radius. A zero knob leaves the parent anchors untouched.
- **Formula:** `new_r[i+1] = new_r[i] + d_i exp(clip(u_i, -u_max, u_max))`
- **Physical effect:** Redistributes wall expansion between anchors.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.0 (source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.h_u0`)
- **Search bounds:** -0.35 .. 0.35 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.h_u0`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.perturb_increments`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/senior_pass_2026-10-24/04_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` § ## WP3. Shape-only perturbations and graded infeasibility

#### `max_wall_deg` — Generator wall-angle cap (deg)

- **Summary:** Upper bound applied to every selected anchor's wall angle.
- **Detail:** The materializer clamps anchor angles into this cap after selecting and perturbing them. The same block key also supplies the default stop for a spherical-salmon march.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Caps how vertical the wall may become.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 80.0 (source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_tractrix.yaml` `generator.max_wall_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.3 `spherical_salmon` (G4)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `mouth` — Mouth cross-section

- **Summary:** Mouth cross-section family: ellipse, superellipse or rounded rectangle.
- **Detail:** The generator emits two cross-section stations: a circular throat and the selected mouth shape. Superellipse and rounded-rectangle mouths read the mouth exponent and corner-radius knobs respectively.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Changes the mouth outline without changing the meridians.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** ellipse (source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_tractrix.yaml` `generator.mouth`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4d. Cross-section stations (crossSections)
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_anchors` — Anchor count

- **Summary:** Number of [z, r, wall-angle] anchors selected per meridian.
- **Detail:** The generator reduces the dense march to this many anchors before the mesher sees it. Both endpoints are always kept and interior anchors are placed by equal arc length in the normalised z/angle plane, so curved regions get more anchors.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises mesh fidelity at the cost of more control points.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 8 (source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_tractrix.yaml` `generator.n_anchors`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.select_anchors`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4b. Profile representation
- **Flags:** —

#### `perturb.mode` — Perturbation mode

- **Summary:** Legacy moves mouth size with the perturbation; shape preserves the parent radius.
- **Detail:** Legacy applies the exponential increments directly, which changes the mouth as well as the wall shape. Shape rescales the perturbed increments so the sum, and therefore the mouth radius, is unchanged. An absent mode behaves as legacy.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Chooses whether the perturbation also changes the mouth size.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** — (source: —)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/senior_pass_2026-10-24/04_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` § ## WP3. Shape-only perturbations and graded infeasibility
- **Flags:** —

#### `perturb.u_max` — Perturbation clip

- **Summary:** Upper clip on each perturbation exponent before increments are scaled.
- **Detail:** The generator clips every u knob into this symmetric limit before scaling the anchor increments. It is a generator-block value, not a searched knob, and it bounds how far a single anchor can move.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Bounds the strongest single-anchor redistribution.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.35 (source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_tractrix.yaml` `generator.perturb.u_max`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.perturb_increments`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/senior_pass_2026-10-24/04_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` § ## WP3. Shape-only perturbations and graded infeasibility
- **Flags:** —

### splice_osse_tractrix — FREEFORM / generator

- **Group:** hybrid
- **Symmetry:** axisymmetric
- **Config:** `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml`
- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/axi_90x90_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** C1 splice OSSE throat -> tractrix mouth, zs_frac searched in [0.2, 0.8] (1 knob)

#### `L_mm` — Depth (mm)

- **Summary:** Axial depth shared by both meridians, throat to mouth.
- **Detail:** The generator builds both meridians to one z span, so L_mm is shared rather than a per-plane length. It is the fixed-length dimension for conical, exponential, hypex and OSSE; length-derived types may only be shortened to it. The brief envelope still bounds the final depth.
- **Formula:** `z = s L, with s the normalised axial fraction`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the mouth plane in or out along the horn axis.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 110.0 (source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.L_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 110.0 .. 110.0 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.L_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.L_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ## 4. FREEFORM
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_first_a_deg` — H-plane OSSE coverage half-angle (first parent) (deg)

- **Summary:** Far-field wall half-angle of the OSSE meridian; the throat angle is the driver exit.
- **Detail:** The OSSE wall starts at the brief's driver exit angle and approaches this coverage half-angle. It is a half-angle in the mesher's convention.
- **Formula:** `OSSE: a is the far-field wall half-angle`
- **Physical effect:** Widens or narrows the OSSE mouth for a fixed depth.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 25.0 (source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.h_first_a_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 25.0 .. 25.0 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.h_first_a_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.a_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `h_first_s` — H-plane termination amplitude (first parent)

- **Summary:** OSSE superellipse termination amplitude; zero disables the termination.
- **Detail:** A nonzero s turns on the OSSE superellipse termination and makes n and q active. The mesher requires a positive n and q while s is nonzero. A zero value leaves the plain OS wall.
- **Formula:** `OSSE superellipse termination (pkg/profile_formulas.py)`
- **Physical effect:** Rolls the mouth toward a superellipse outline instead of a plain OS wall.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.4 (source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.h_first_s`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.4 .. 0.4 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.h_first_s`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.s`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry._osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_second_fc_hz` — H-plane cutoff frequency (second parent) (Hz)

- **Summary:** Plane-wave cutoff that fixes the flare constant or the tractrix mouth size.
- **Detail:** Tractrix and the Le Cleac'h family size their mouth from this cutoff; the exponential family derives its flare constant from it. It is a frequency, not a wall angle, and it does not change the brief's analysis band.
- **Formula:** `m = 4 pi fc / c (Le Cleac'h); a = c / (2 pi fc) (tractrix mouth radius)`
- **Physical effect:** Raises or lowers the corner around which the horn stops loading.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 750.0 (source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.h_second_fc_hz`)
- **Search bounds:** 750.0 .. 750.0 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.h_second_fc_hz`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1a. Mathematical formula
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_zs_frac` — H-plane splice position

- **Summary:** Splice location as a fraction of the first parent's meridian length.
- **Detail:** A splice joins two parents built from the same throat. zs_frac places the joint along the first parent; the second parent is translated so its radius matches at that point, and a short bridge is used when the wall angles disagree.
- **Formula:** `z_s = zs_frac * first.length_mm`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the joint between the throat-side and mouth-side parent walls.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.5 (source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `seed_params.h_zs_frac`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.2 .. 0.8 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `bounds.h_zs_frac`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.splice`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.1 `splice` (G2)
- **Flags:** —

#### `max_wall_deg` — Generator wall-angle cap (deg)

- **Summary:** Upper bound applied to every selected anchor's wall angle.
- **Detail:** The materializer clamps anchor angles into this cap after selecting and perturbing them. The same block key also supplies the default stop for a spherical-salmon march.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Caps how vertical the wall may become.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 80.0 (source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `generator.max_wall_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.3 `spherical_salmon` (G4)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `mouth` — Mouth cross-section

- **Summary:** Mouth cross-section family: ellipse, superellipse or rounded rectangle.
- **Detail:** The generator emits two cross-section stations: a circular throat and the selected mouth shape. Superellipse and rounded-rectangle mouths read the mouth exponent and corner-radius knobs respectively.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Changes the mouth outline without changing the meridians.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** ellipse (source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `generator.mouth`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4d. Cross-section stations (crossSections)
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_anchors` — Anchor count

- **Summary:** Number of [z, r, wall-angle] anchors selected per meridian.
- **Detail:** The generator reduces the dense march to this many anchors before the mesher sees it. Both endpoints are always kept and interior anchors are placed by equal arc length in the normalised z/angle plane, so curved regions get more anchors.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises mesh fidelity at the cost of more control points.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 16 (source: `examples/typologies/splice_osse_tractrix.yaml` `generator.n_anchors`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.select_anchors`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4b. Profile representation
- **Flags:** —

## Group: legacy

### search_smoke — OSSE / native

- **Group:** legacy
- **Symmetry:** hv
- **Config:** `examples/search_smoke.yaml`
- **Brief:** —
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** Small smoke-test copy of osse_baseline with clipped budget; not for typology comparison

#### `L_mm` — OSSE main-section length (mm)

- **Summary:** Length of the main OSSE section; extension and slot may add to the total.
- **Detail:** The mesher requires it positive. In the total-length mode the section is carved differently, so the same value can mean a different total span.
- **Formula:** `z_main in [0, L]`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the depth of the flared OSSE body.
- **Context:** `native:osse`
- **Default:** 260.0 (source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `seed_params.L_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 140.0 .. 420.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `bounds.L_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.L_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `a0_deg` — Throat half-angle (deg)

- **Summary:** Wall half-angle at the throat, passed to the OSSE, R-OSSE and ICW meridian.
- **Detail:** OSSE carries it in the 2 k r0 z tan(a0) term of the base radius, and R-OSSE carries the same term in its throat-radius curve. ICW uses it as the initial tangent angle theta0 of the curvature integral. It should match the driver exit angle when the wall is meant to continue the driver.
- **Formula:** `OSSE/R-OSSE: c2 = 2 k r0 tan(a0); ICW: theta(0) = theta0`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the initial expansion slope at the driver exit.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 15.5 (source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `seed_params.a0_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 2.0 .. 30.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `bounds.a0_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.a0_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `a_deg` — Native coverage half-angle (deg)

- **Summary:** OSSE/R-OSSE coverage half-angle in the mesher's own convention.
- **Detail:** The mesher measures the angle as a half-angle from the axis. The target block in a config measures the total included angle, which is the value the objective scores. Read the block before comparing the two.
- **Formula:** `OSSE: base(z) carries tan(a); R-OSSE: c3 = tan(a)^2`
- **Physical effect:** Sets how wide the wall opens relative to the axis.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 37.5 (source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `seed_params.a_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 5.0 .. 55.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `bounds.a_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.a_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `aspect_ratio` — Cross-section aspect ratio

- **Summary:** Width-to-height scaling of the cross-section relative to the meridian radius.
- **Detail:** The mesher scales the horizontal and vertical half-widths of the outline by this ratio. A value of one gives a circular section; larger values widen it. It does not change either meridian and is not a coverage angle.
- **Formula:** `b = a * aspect_ratio in the ellipse/superellipse station`
- **Physical effect:** Widens or narrows the cross-section without changing the wall profiles.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 1.25 (source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `seed_params.aspect_ratio`)
- **Search bounds:** 1.0 .. 2.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `bounds.aspect_ratio`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=cross_section (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.aspect_ratio`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.families.FAMILY_KEYS`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ## 7. Geometric / cross-section model (all formulas)
- **Flags:** —

#### `exponent` — Native cross-section exponent

- **Summary:** Superellipse exponent of the outline; the documented default is disputed.
- **Detail:** The research basis states a default of 2.0 (a plain ellipse); the shipped generator default for the same idea is 4.0 (registry.DEFAULT_KNOBS). This entry records both claims and does not choose between them. It is not the generator's mouth_exponent key, although the two describe the same idea.
- **Formula:** `( |cos(phi)/a|^e + |sin(phi)/b|^e )^(-1/e)`
- **Physical effect:** Squares off or rounds the cross-section outline.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 4.0 (source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `seed_params.exponent`)
- **Search bounds:** 1.0 .. 8.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `bounds.exponent`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=cross_section (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.exponent`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.families.FAMILY_KEYS`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ## 4. The geometry we would have written already exists
- **Flags:** doc_mismatch, overloaded

#### `k` — OS-SE shape factor

- **Summary:** Blends the OS-SE base radius between its radius term and its tangent term.
- **Detail:** The base radius carries (k r0) and r0(1-k) terms plus the tangent-driven z term, so k reshapes the wall between the throat and the far-field angle. R-OSSE reuses it in its throat-radius curve. It is dimensionless and is not a termination control.
- **Formula:** `base(z) = sqrt((k r0)^2 + 2 k r0 z tan(a0) + z^2 tan(a)^2) + r0(1-k)`
- **Physical effect:** Reshapes the wall between the throat and the far-field angle.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 1.0 (source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `seed_params.k`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.3 .. 2.5 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `bounds.k`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.k`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `n` — OSSE termination exponent

- **Summary:** Superellipse exponent of the OSSE termination term.
- **Detail:** It is read only while the termination amplitude is nonzero. The biradial generator meaning of n is not asserted in this context.
- **Formula:** `term = (s L/q)(1 - (1 - z_norm^n)^(1/n))`
- **Physical effect:** Sharpens or softens the OSSE mouth roll.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 4.0 (source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `seed_params.n`)
- **Search bounds:** 2.0 .. 8.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `bounds.n`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.n`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `q` — OSSE termination saturation

- **Summary:** Superellipse saturation of the OSSE termination; positive while s is nonzero.
- **Detail:** It sets how quickly the termination term saturates with axial station. The mesher requires it positive whenever the termination amplitude s is nonzero.
- **Formula:** `z_norm = q z/L; term = (s L/q)(1 - (1 - z_norm^n)^(1/n))`
- **Physical effect:** Changes how abruptly the terminated outline reaches its corner.
- **Context:** `native:osse`
- **Default:** 0.995 (source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `seed_params.q`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.9 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `bounds.q`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.q`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `r0_mm` — Throat radius (mm)

- **Summary:** Throat radius every native family starts its meridian from.
- **Detail:** OSSE and R-OSSE use it as the base radius of the wall law, ICW uses it as the throat integration constant, and a FREEFORM meridian starts at its own first anchor radius. It is shared by the H and V planes unless a per-azimuth expression is used. The mesher rejects a non-positive value.
- **Formula:** `OSSE: base(z) starts at k r0 + r0(1-k); ICW: r(s) = r0 + S integral sin(theta)`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the throat area and the driver exit the wall must match.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 17.78 (source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `seed_params.r0_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 17.78 .. 17.78 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `bounds.r0_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.r0_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `s` — Termination amplitude

- **Summary:** OSSE superellipse termination amplitude; zero leaves the plain OS wall.
- **Detail:** A nonzero amplitude turns on the superellipse termination and makes n and q active. A zero value leaves the plain OS wall. R-OSSE has no s; its rollback comes from the blended curves and tmax, not from this key.
- **Formula:** `term is added to base only while s != 0`
- **Physical effect:** Rolls the OSSE mouth toward a superellipse outline.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 0.7 (source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `seed_params.s`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/search_smoke.yaml` `bounds.s`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.s`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

- **Search bounds:** 17.78 .. 17.78 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/throat_adapter.yaml` `bounds.r0_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.r0_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `s` — Termination amplitude

- **Summary:** OSSE superellipse termination amplitude; zero leaves the plain OS wall.
- **Detail:** A nonzero amplitude turns on the superellipse termination and makes n and q active. A zero value leaves the plain OS wall. R-OSSE has no s; its rollback comes from the blended curves and tmax, not from this key.
- **Formula:** `term is added to base only while s != 0`
- **Physical effect:** Rolls the OSSE mouth toward a superellipse outline.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 0.7 (source: `examples/throat_adapter.yaml` `seed_params.s`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.7 .. 0.7 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/throat_adapter.yaml` `bounds.s`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.s`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `slot_length_mm` — Searched cylindrical spacer (mm)

- **Summary:** This config searches a straight slot between the extension and the main horn.
- **Detail:** The slot holds the throat radius constant over its length. A zero length removes it.
- **Formula:** `r = r0_main over [ext_len, ext_len + slot_len]`
- **Physical effect:** Adds a cylindrical spacer in the throat region.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 0.0 (source: `examples/throat_adapter.yaml` `seed_params.slot_length_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 30.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/throat_adapter.yaml` `bounds.slot_length_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.slot_length_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 6e. Throat extensions and slots (OSSE, R-OSSE)
- **Flags:** —

#### `throat_ext_angle_deg` — Searched adapter taper (deg)

- **Summary:** This config searches the extension taper angle.
- **Detail:** The taper changes how the adapter radius meets the main throat radius. It is not the main horn's coverage angle.
- **Formula:** `r0_throat = r0 - ext_len tan(throat_ext_angle)`
- **Physical effect:** Narrows or widens the adapter toward the driver.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 5.0 (source: `examples/throat_adapter.yaml` `seed_params.throat_ext_angle_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 15.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/throat_adapter.yaml` `bounds.throat_ext_angle_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.throat_ext_angle_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 6e. Throat extensions and slots (OSSE, R-OSSE)
- **Flags:** —

#### `throat_ext_length_mm` — Searched adapter length (mm)

- **Summary:** This config searches the prepended extension length.
- **Detail:** The extension is the typology's point: it moves the driver interface ahead of the main OSSE wall. A zero length removes it.
- **Formula:** `r = r0_throat + z tan(throat_ext_angle), r0_throat = r0 - ext_len tan(angle)`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the start of the horn body away from the driver.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 10.0 (source: `examples/throat_adapter.yaml` `seed_params.throat_ext_length_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 40.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/throat_adapter.yaml` `bounds.throat_ext_length_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.throat_ext_length_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 6e. Throat extensions and slots (OSSE, R-OSSE)
- **Flags:** —

## Group: magnus

### freeform_hv — FREEFORM / native

- **Group:** magnus
- **Symmetry:** hv
- **Config:** `examples/freeform_hv.yaml`
- **Brief:** —
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** FREEFORM H/V waveguide 75x45 deg, 3 searchable knobs (mouth angles + exponent)

#### `crossSections` — Circle-to-superellipse schedule

- **Summary:** The schedule is fixed: a circular throat station and a superellipse mouth station.
- **Detail:** The schedule itself is not searched in this config; only its exponent is. The blend between stations is smootherstep.
- **Formula:** `r_3D(t, phi) = r_meridian(t) * station_shape(phi)`
- **Physical effect:** Morphs the outline from round to squared.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** [{'t': 0.0, 'shape': 'circle'}, {'t': 1.0, 'shape': 'superellipse', 'exponent': 4.0}] (source: `examples/freeform_hv.yaml` `seed_params.crossSections`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=list, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.FREEFORM.crossSections`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.freeform.build_freeform_geometry`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4d. Cross-section stations (crossSections)
- **Flags:** not_a_knob

#### `exponent` — Alias into the superellipse station

- **Summary:** The YAML bounds a bare exponent that resolves into the superellipse station.
- **Detail:** families.FREEFORM_ALIASES routes the name to the first superellipse station. The station's own field is what the mesher reads.
- **Formula:** `( |cos(phi)/a|^e + |sin(phi)/b|^e )^(-1/e)`
- **Physical effect:** Squares off the mouth cross-section.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 4.0 (source: `examples/freeform_hv.yaml` `seed_params.exponent`)
- **Search bounds:** 2.0 .. 8.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/freeform_hv.yaml` `bounds.exponent`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.families.FAMILY_KEYS`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ## 4. The geometry we would have written already exists
- **Flags:** doc_mismatch, overloaded

#### `profileH` — Only the mouth angle is searched here

- **Summary:** The horizontal anchors are pinned; the config searches the horizontal mouth angle.
- **Detail:** Because the points do not move, this typology explores wall exit angle rather than control-point radii. The pinned throat keeps the driver match.
- **Formula:** `cubic Hermite through the H anchors with endpoint tangent angles`
- **Physical effect:** Changes the horizontal wall angle at the mouth.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** {'points': [[0.0, 12.7], [60.0, 80.0], [120.0, 160.0]], 'throatAngleDeg': 15.5, 'mouthAngleDeg': 70.0} (source: `examples/freeform_hv.yaml` `seed_params.profileH`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=dict, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.FREEFORM.profileH`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.freeform.build_freeform_geometry`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4b. Profile representation
- **Flags:** not_a_knob

#### `profileH.mouthAngleDeg` — Only the mouth angle is searched here [profileH.mouthAngleDeg]

- **Detail:** Because the points do not move, this typology explores wall exit angle rather than control-point radii. The pinned throat keeps the driver match.

- **Search bounds:** 40.0 .. 90.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/freeform_hv.yaml` `bounds.profileH.mouthAngleDeg`)

### freeform_sculpt — FREEFORM / native

- **Group:** magnus
- **Symmetry:** hv
- **Config:** `examples/freeform_sculpt.yaml`
- **Brief:** —
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** FREEFORM sculpted waveguide 90x90 deg, 11 searchable control-point knobs

#### `crossSections` — Circle-to-superellipse schedule

- **Summary:** This config runs a circular throat section to a superellipse mouth section.
- **Detail:** The schedule has a throat station and a mouth station, and the searched exponent lands in the superellipse station. The smootherstep blend between the two stations is the mesher's job.
- **Formula:** `r_3D(t, phi) = r_meridian(t) * station_shape(phi)`
- **Physical effect:** Morphs the outline from round at the throat to squared at the mouth.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** [{'t': 0.0, 'shape': 'circle'}, {'t': 1.0, 'shape': 'superellipse', 'exponent': 4.0}] (source: `examples/freeform_sculpt.yaml` `seed_params.crossSections`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=list, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.FREEFORM.crossSections`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.freeform.build_freeform_geometry`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4d. Cross-section stations (crossSections)
- **Flags:** not_a_knob

#### `exponent` — Alias into the superellipse station

- **Summary:** The searched exponent is the superellipse station's field, reached through the FREEFORM alias.
- **Detail:** families.py resolves a bare exponent for FREEFORM to the first superellipse station in crossSections. It is not a top-level family key in the config even though the YAML bounds it by that name.
- **Formula:** `( |cos(phi)/a|^e + |sin(phi)/b|^e )^(-1/e)`
- **Physical effect:** Squares off the mouth cross-section.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 4.0 (source: `examples/freeform_sculpt.yaml` `seed_params.exponent`)
- **Search bounds:** 2.0 .. 8.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/freeform_sculpt.yaml` `bounds.exponent`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.families.FAMILY_KEYS`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ## 4. The geometry we would have written already exists
- **Flags:** doc_mismatch, overloaded

#### `profileH` — Control-point radii are the search dimensions

- **Summary:** The horizontal interior anchor radii are searched; z values and the throat are pinned.
- **Detail:** Only radius fields move, so z stays strictly increasing and the H/V span stays equal. That is why this config can sculpt the wall without breaking the FREEFORM guards.
- **Formula:** `cubic Hermite through the H anchors with endpoint tangent angles`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the horizontal wall at the interior anchors.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** {'points': [[0.0, 12.7], [45.0, 38.0], [95.0, 80.0], [145.0, 130.0], [195.0, 185.0]], 'throatAngleDeg': 10.0, 'mouthAngleDeg': 70.0} (source: `examples/freeform_sculpt.yaml` `seed_params.profileH`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=dict, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.FREEFORM.profileH`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.freeform.build_freeform_geometry`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4b. Profile representation
- **Flags:** not_a_knob

- **Formula:** `cubic Hermite through the V anchors with endpoint tangent angles`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the vertical wall at the interior anchors.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 185.0 (source: `examples/freeform_sculpt.yaml` `seed_params.profileV.points.4.1`)
- **Search bounds:** 163.0 .. 200.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/freeform_sculpt.yaml` `bounds.profileV.points.4.1`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=dict, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.FREEFORM.profileV`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.freeform.build_freeform_geometry`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4b. Profile representation

### icw_rollback — ICW / native

- **Group:** magnus
- **Symmetry:** axisymmetric
- **Config:** `examples/icw_rollback.yaml`
- **Brief:** —
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** ICW rollback (icw_seed from R-OSSE, tmax=1.0 natural rollback), 1-inch driver; 5 searchable knobs

#### `icw_seed` — Rollback comes from the seed

- **Summary:** This config fits an ICW curve to a rolled-back R-OSSE seed rather than solving targets.
- **Detail:** The nested seed carries a full R-OSSE curve whose wall turns past vertical, and the ICW B-spline inherits that rollback. The searched keys live inside the nested seed, not at the ICW top level.
- **Formula:** `fit_from_points(sample(seed_from_osse or seed_from_rosse))`
- **Physical effect:** Starts the ICW curve from a naturally rolled-back meridian.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** {'type': 'R-OSSE', 'r0_mm': 12.7, 'a0_deg': 5.0, 'a_deg': 45.0, 'R_mm': 160.0, 'tmax': 1.0, 'k': 1.0, 'q': 1.0, 'm': 0.85, 'r': 0.4, 'b': 0.2, 'exponent': 4.0} (source: `examples/icw_rollback.yaml` `seed_params.icw_seed`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=dict, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.icw_seed`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.build_icw_curve`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 3b. ICW config parameters
