### icw_termination — ICW / native

- **Group:** magnus
- **Symmetry:** axisymmetric
- **Config:** `examples/icw_termination.yaml`
- **Brief:** —
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** ICW flat-baffle termination, 100x100 deg, 1-inch driver; 6 searchable knobs

#### `L_mm` — ICW axial depth (mm)

- **Summary:** Axial depth x(1) targeted by an ICW flat-baffle solve.
- **Detail:** It is a size target, not an arc length: the ICW solver finds the arc length and shape that reach this depth. Direct-coefficient mode uses icw_S for the arc length instead.
- **Formula:** `x(1) = L`
- **Physical effect:** Sets how deep the ICW meridian runs.
- **Context:** `native:icw`
- **Default:** 160.0 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.L_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 160.0 .. 160.0 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `bounds.L_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.L_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `R_mm` — ICW mouth radius (mm)

- **Summary:** Mouth radius r(1) targeted by an ICW flat-baffle solve.
- **Detail:** It is a size target of the solve, not an integration constant. The ICW solver finds a curve that reaches it.
- **Formula:** `r(1) = R`
- **Physical effect:** Sets how wide the ICW mouth is solved to be.
- **Context:** `native:icw`
- **Default:** 130.0 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.R_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 80.0 .. 220.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `bounds.R_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.R_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_rosse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 2b. R-OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `a0_deg` — Throat half-angle (deg)

- **Summary:** Wall half-angle at the throat, passed to the OSSE, R-OSSE and ICW meridian.
- **Detail:** OSSE carries it in the 2 k r0 z tan(a0) term of the base radius, and R-OSSE carries the same term in its throat-radius curve. ICW uses it as the initial tangent angle theta0 of the curvature integral. It should match the driver exit angle when the wall is meant to continue the driver.
- **Formula:** `OSSE/R-OSSE: c2 = 2 k r0 tan(a0); ICW: theta(0) = theta0`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the initial expansion slope at the driver exit.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 14.5 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.a0_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 14.5 .. 14.5 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `bounds.a0_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.a0_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `coverage_angle_deg` — Half-angle in this config (deg)

- **Summary:** This is the ICW wall half-angle; the target coverage is the total included angle.
- **Detail:** The mesher requires it between the throat angle and vertical. The target block scores the full included angle, so the two are not numerically equal.
- **Formula:** `theta(sigma) = coverage_angle_deg over [hold_start, hold_end]`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the wall angle held through the coverage plateau.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 50.0 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.coverage_angle_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 20.0 .. 80.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `bounds.coverage_angle_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=0.0, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.coverage_angle_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.build_icw_curve`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 3b. ICW config parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `dkappa_ds_abs_max` — Curvature-slope cap

- **Summary:** Cap on the sampled maximum absolute curvature derivative along normalised arc length.
- **Detail:** The mesher describes the cap as max|dkappa/dsigma| and gives its own wording for the unit, which does not settle a single unit token. This layer therefore leaves the unit unset instead of inventing one. The ICW dossier records it as a solver cap beside kappa_abs_max.
- **Formula:** `max|dkappa/dsigma| <= dkappa_ds_abs_max`
- **Physical effect:** Limits how quickly the ICW wall's curvature may change.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 10.0 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.dkappa_ds_abs_max`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.dkappa_ds_abs_max`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.build_icw_curve`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 3c. ICW solver logic (pkg/icw/solver.py)
- **Flags:** —

#### `hold_end` — Coverage plateau end here

- **Summary:** Fraction of arc length where this config's constant-angle plateau ends.
- **Detail:** It is a fraction, not a length, and must stay above hold_start. It is active because this config uses coverage mode.
- **Formula:** `sigma_hold_end = hold_end`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the plateau end along the meridian.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 0.7 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.hold_end`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.55 .. 0.9 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `bounds.hold_end`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=0.0, hi=1.0, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.hold_end`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.build_icw_curve`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 3b. ICW config parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `hold_start` — Coverage plateau start here

- **Summary:** Fraction of arc length where this config's constant-angle plateau begins.
- **Detail:** It is a fraction, not a length, and must stay below hold_end. It is active because this config uses coverage mode.
- **Formula:** `sigma_hold_start = hold_start`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the plateau start along the meridian.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 0.3 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.hold_start`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.1 .. 0.45 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `bounds.hold_start`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=0.0, hi=1.0, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.hold_start`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.build_icw_curve`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 3b. ICW config parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `kappa0` — Throat curvature (1/mm)

- **Summary:** Meridional curvature at the throat of the ICW curve.
- **Detail:** The ICW curve integrates curvature to get the tangent angle, so kappa0 sets the initial turn rate. It is a signed curvature in inverse millimetres. A zero value starts the curve locally straight.
- **Formula:** `kappa(sigma) = sum_i a_i B_i(sigma); kappa(0) = kappa0`
- **Physical effect:** Bends the wall immediately after the throat.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 0.25 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.kappa0`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 0.5 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `bounds.kappa0`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.kappa0`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.build_icw_curve`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 3b. ICW config parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `kappa_abs_max` — Curvature cap (1/mm)

- **Summary:** Cap on the sampled maximum absolute meridional curvature.
- **Detail:** It is a soft barrier in the feasible-subspace solve, not a hard geometric clamp. It is in inverse millimetres like kappa0. If unset the solver has no curvature cap.
- **Formula:** `max|kappa| <= kappa_abs_max`
- **Physical effect:** Limits how tightly the ICW wall may bend.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 1.0 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.kappa_abs_max`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.kappa_abs_max`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.build_icw_curve`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 3b. ICW config parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `r0_mm` — Throat radius (mm)

- **Summary:** Throat radius every native family starts its meridian from.
- **Detail:** OSSE and R-OSSE use it as the base radius of the wall law, ICW uses it as the throat integration constant, and a FREEFORM meridian starts at its own first anchor radius. It is shared by the H and V planes unless a per-azimuth expression is used. The mesher rejects a non-positive value.
- **Formula:** `OSSE: base(z) starts at k r0 + r0(1-k); ICW: r(s) = r0 + S integral sin(theta)`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the throat area and the driver exit the wall must match.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 12.7 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.r0_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 12.7 .. 12.7 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `bounds.r0_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.r0_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `termination` — Flat baffle in this config

- **Summary:** termination selects flat_baffle, so the L/R/coverage/hold targets are active.
- **Detail:** This typology is the flat-baffle ICW route. The rollback keys are not the active termination controls here.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Stops the wall at a flat mouth.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** flat_baffle (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.termination`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=str, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.termination`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.build_icw_curve`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 3b. ICW config parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `theta1_deg` — Present but not the active termination (deg)

- **Summary:** theta1_deg is a rollback target and is not read in this flat-baffle config.
- **Detail:** The seed carries a bounded theta1 value, but termination is flat_baffle, so the rollback terminal angle is not the law being applied. Do not describe it as this typology's mouth control.
- **Formula:** `theta(1) = theta1_deg (rollback targets)`
- **Physical effect:** Would set a rollback terminal angle if rollback mode were selected.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 16.0 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.theta1_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 2.0 .. 30.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `bounds.theta1_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.theta1_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.build_icw_curve`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 3b. ICW config parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `theta_max_deg` — Rollback wall-angle cap (deg)

- **Summary:** Rollback-only cap on the wall angle, above vertical and at most a half turn.
- **Detail:** The mesher applies it to rollback solves only. It bounds how far around the turn the solve may go. Flat-baffle mode ignores it.
- **Formula:** `max(theta) <= theta_max_deg (rollback only)`
- **Physical effect:** Bounds how far the rollback may curl around.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 91.0 (source: `examples/icw_termination.yaml` `seed_params.theta_max_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.theta_max_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.build_icw_curve`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 3b. ICW config parameters
- **Flags:** —

### osse_baseline — OSSE / native

- **Group:** magnus
- **Symmetry:** hv
- **Config:** `examples/osse_baseline.yaml`
- **Brief:** —
- **Source status:** catalog-brief-tied
- **Description:** OSSE baseline 75x45 deg, 1.4-inch driver, 10 searchable knobs (CP-8c reference)

#### `L_mm` — OSSE main-section length (mm)

- **Summary:** Length of the main OSSE section; extension and slot may add to the total.
- **Detail:** The mesher requires it positive. In the total-length mode the section is carved differently, so the same value can mean a different total span.
- **Formula:** `z_main in [0, L]`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the depth of the flared OSSE body.
- **Context:** `native:osse`
- **Default:** 260.0 (source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.L_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 140.0 .. 420.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.L_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.L_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `a0_deg` — Throat half-angle (deg)

- **Summary:** Wall half-angle at the throat, passed to the OSSE, R-OSSE and ICW meridian.
- **Detail:** OSSE carries it in the 2 k r0 z tan(a0) term of the base radius, and R-OSSE carries the same term in its throat-radius curve. ICW uses it as the initial tangent angle theta0 of the curvature integral. It should match the driver exit angle when the wall is meant to continue the driver.
- **Formula:** `OSSE/R-OSSE: c2 = 2 k r0 tan(a0); ICW: theta(0) = theta0`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the initial expansion slope at the driver exit.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 15.5 (source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.a0_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 2.0 .. 30.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.a0_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.a0_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `a_deg` — Half-angle in this config (deg)

- **Summary:** This config's a_deg is the mesher half-angle; the target coverage is the total angle.
- **Detail:** The target block scores the full included angle, so the two numbers differ by the same factor of two that the OSSE convention implies. Do not copy a target coverage value into a_deg without halving it.
- **Formula:** `OSSE: base(z) carries tan(a); R-OSSE: c3 = tan(a)^2`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the OSSE coverage half-angle.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 37.5 (source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.a_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 5.0 .. 55.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.a_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.a_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `aspect_ratio` — Cross-section aspect ratio

- **Summary:** Width-to-height scaling of the cross-section relative to the meridian radius.
- **Detail:** The mesher scales the horizontal and vertical half-widths of the outline by this ratio. A value of one gives a circular section; larger values widen it. It does not change either meridian and is not a coverage angle.
- **Formula:** `b = a * aspect_ratio in the ellipse/superellipse station`
- **Physical effect:** Widens or narrows the cross-section without changing the wall profiles.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 1.25 (source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.aspect_ratio`)
- **Search bounds:** 1.0 .. 2.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.aspect_ratio`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=cross_section (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.aspect_ratio`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.families.FAMILY_KEYS`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ## 7. Geometric / cross-section model (all formulas)
- **Flags:** —

#### `exponent` — Family-level cross-section exponent

- **Summary:** Here exponent is the OSSE-family cross-section key, not a FREEFORM station field.
- **Detail:** It pairs with aspect_ratio at the family level. The shipped seed uses the higher value that the prose/code mismatch records, so read the entry's doc_mismatch note before treating either value as canonical.
- **Formula:** `( |cos(phi)/a|^e + |sin(phi)/b|^e )^(-1/e)`
- **Physical effect:** Squares off the OSSE cross-section outline.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 4.0 (source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.exponent`)
- **Search bounds:** 1.0 .. 8.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.exponent`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=cross_section (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.exponent`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.families.FAMILY_KEYS`
- **Doc:** `docs/plan/12_RESEARCH_BASIS.md` § ## 4. The geometry we would have written already exists
- **Flags:** doc_mismatch, overloaded

#### `k` — OS-SE shape factor

- **Summary:** Blends the OS-SE base radius between its radius term and its tangent term.
- **Detail:** The base radius carries (k r0) and r0(1-k) terms plus the tangent-driven z term, so k reshapes the wall between the throat and the far-field angle. R-OSSE reuses it in its throat-radius curve. It is dimensionless and is not a termination control.
- **Formula:** `base(z) = sqrt((k r0)^2 + 2 k r0 z tan(a0) + z^2 tan(a)^2) + r0(1-k)`
- **Physical effect:** Reshapes the wall between the throat and the far-field angle.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 1.0 (source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.k`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.3 .. 2.5 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.k`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.k`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `n` — OSSE termination exponent

- **Summary:** Superellipse exponent of the OSSE termination term.
- **Detail:** It is read only while the termination amplitude is nonzero. The biradial generator meaning of n is not asserted in this context.
- **Formula:** `term = (s L/q)(1 - (1 - z_norm^n)^(1/n))`
- **Physical effect:** Sharpens or softens the OSSE mouth roll.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 4.0 (source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.n`)
- **Search bounds:** 2.0 .. 8.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.n`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.n`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `q` — OSSE termination saturation

- **Summary:** Superellipse saturation of the OSSE termination; positive while s is nonzero.
- **Detail:** It sets how quickly the termination term saturates with axial station. The mesher requires it positive whenever the termination amplitude s is nonzero.
- **Formula:** `z_norm = q z/L; term = (s L/q)(1 - (1 - z_norm^n)^(1/n))`
- **Physical effect:** Changes how abruptly the terminated outline reaches its corner.
- **Context:** `native:osse`
- **Default:** 0.995 (source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.q`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.9 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.q`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.q`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `r0_mm` — Throat radius (mm)

- **Summary:** Throat radius every native family starts its meridian from.
- **Detail:** OSSE and R-OSSE use it as the base radius of the wall law, ICW uses it as the throat integration constant, and a FREEFORM meridian starts at its own first anchor radius. It is shared by the H and V planes unless a per-azimuth expression is used. The mesher rejects a non-positive value.
- **Formula:** `OSSE: base(z) starts at k r0 + r0(1-k); ICW: r(s) = r0 + S integral sin(theta)`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the throat area and the driver exit the wall must match.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 17.78 (source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.r0_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 17.78 .. 17.78 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.r0_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.r0_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `s` — Termination enabled here

- **Summary:** This seed enables the OSSE termination, so n and q are live.
- **Detail:** The seed carries a nonzero termination amplitude. That is why n and q are not inert in this typology even though they are conditional keys.
- **Formula:** `term is added to base only while s != 0`
- **Physical effect:** Turns the OSSE mouth termination on.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 0.7 (source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.s`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/osse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.s`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.s`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

### rosse_baseline — R-OSSE / native

#### `R_mm` — Reference radius in this config (mm)

- **Summary:** R_mm is the nominal mouth/reference radius; this family derives its axial length.
- **Detail:** R-OSSE refuses an explicit L_mm, so R_mm plus the shape keys set both the mouth and the derived depth. Do not look for a separate length knob here.
- **Formula:** `mouth_r(t) = max(0, R + L(1 - sqrt(1 + c3(t-1)^2)))`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the mouth size and the derived depth together.
- **Context:** `native:rosse`
- **Default:** 155.0 (source: `examples/rosse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.R_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 80.0 .. 220.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/rosse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.R_mm`)

- **Formula:** `OSSE/R-OSSE: c2 = 2 k r0 tan(a0); ICW: theta(0) = theta0`

- **Detail:** The mesher scales the horizontal and vertical half-widths of the outline by this ratio. A value of one gives a circular section; larger values widen it. It does not change either meridian and is not a coverage angle.
- **Formula:** `b = a * aspect_ratio in the ellipse/superellipse station`

#### `b` — R-OSSE secondary blend

- **Summary:** Weight of the secondary quadratic axial term in the R-OSSE map.
- **Detail:** The term multiplies t^2 and a difference of two square roots. A zero value removes the secondary term. It is dimensionless and is R-OSSE-only.
- **Formula:** `x(t) += b L(sqrt(r^2+(1-m)^2) - sqrt(r^2+m^2)) t^2`
- **Physical effect:** Bends the axial map toward or away from its secondary shape.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 0.2 (source: `examples/rosse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.b`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 0.5 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/rosse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.b`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.R-OSSE.b`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_rosse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 2a. Mathematical formula
- **Flags:** —

#### `k` — OS-SE shape factor

- **Summary:** Blends the OS-SE base radius between its radius term and its tangent term.
- **Detail:** The base radius carries (k r0) and r0(1-k) terms plus the tangent-driven z term, so k reshapes the wall between the throat and the far-field angle. R-OSSE reuses it in its throat-radius curve. It is dimensionless and is not a termination control.
- **Formula:** `base(z) = sqrt((k r0)^2 + 2 k r0 z tan(a0) + z^2 tan(a)^2) + r0(1-k)`
- **Physical effect:** Reshapes the wall between the throat and the far-field angle.
- **Context:** `native`

- **Flags:** —

#### `m` — R-OSSE axial shape m

- **Summary:** Shape parameter inside the hyperbolic axial map of R-OSSE.
- **Detail:** The axial map carries sqrt(r^2 + m^2) and sqrt(r^2 + (t-m)^2), so m moves the map's shoulder along the parameter. It is dimensionless and does not change the radius curves directly. It is R-OSSE-only.
- **Formula:** `x(t) = L(sqrt(r^2+m^2) - sqrt(r^2+(t-m)^2)) + b L(sqrt(r^2+(1-m)^2) - sqrt(r^2+m^2)) t^2`
- **Physical effect:** Moves how axial distance accumulates along the R-OSSE curve.
- **Context:** `native`
- **Default:** 0.85 (source: `examples/rosse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.m`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.3 .. 1.5 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/rosse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.m`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.R-OSSE.m`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_rosse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 2a. Mathematical formula
- **Flags:** —

#### `q` — Blend exponent in this config

- **Summary:** In this R-OSSE config q is the throat-to-mouth blend exponent, not the OSSE saturation.
- **Detail:** The seed documents the R-OSSE default as the blend value. Reading q as a superellipse saturation here would describe the wrong equation.
- **Formula:** `r(t) = (1 - t^q) throat_r(t) + t^q mouth_r(t)`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the blend hand-over between throat and mouth curves.
- **Context:** `native:rosse`
- **Default:** 1.0 (source: `examples/rosse_baseline.yaml` `seed_params.q`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.5 .. 2.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/rosse_baseline.yaml` `bounds.q`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.R-OSSE.q`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `r` — R-OSSE axial curve radius r

- **Summary:** Rounding radius of the hyperbolic axial map of R-OSSE.
- **Detail:** The parameter r appears under the square roots of the axial map. A larger value smooths the map and reduces the corner near the shoulder. It is dimensionless and is not a physical radius.
- **Formula:** `sqrt(r^2 + m^2), sqrt(r^2 + (t-m)^2), sqrt(r^2 + (1-m)^2)`

- **Code:** `hornlab_mesher.profile_formulas.calculate_rosse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 2a. Mathematical formula
- **Flags:** —

#### `r0_mm` — Throat radius (mm)

- **Summary:** Throat radius every native family starts its meridian from.
- **Detail:** OSSE and R-OSSE use it as the base radius of the wall law, ICW uses it as the throat integration constant, and a FREEFORM meridian starts at its own first anchor radius. It is shared by the H and V planes unless a per-azimuth expression is used. The mesher rejects a non-positive value.
- **Formula:** `OSSE: base(z) starts at k r0 + r0(1-k); ICW: r(s) = r0 + S integral sin(theta)`

#### `tmax` — Full curve in this config

- **Summary:** tmax is left at its full value, so the wall reaches the blended mouth.
- **Detail:** At full truncation the wall can pass vertical and roll back naturally. A smaller tmax would stop it before the mouth.
- **Formula:** `t in [0, tmax]`
- **Physical effect:** Lets the natural R-OSSE rollback run to the mouth.

### lecleach_asym_odd — FREEFORM / rows

- **Group:** organic
- **Symmetry:** full
- **Config:** `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml`
- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/lecleach_rollback_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** Le Cleac'h (Fourcade 2015) march + odd-cosine one-sided asymmetry, full ring (5 knobs); labeled reconstruction

#### `g_T` — Rows family factor (rows)

- **Summary:** Salmon family T on the Salmon rows; Fourcade area-law T on the Le Cleac'h rows.
- **Detail:** The two row families share the spelling but not the equation. The Salmon rows use it in the family area law; the verified Le Cleac'h rows use it in the Fourcade wavefront-area law.
- **Formula:** `Salmon family law; Fourcade eq. 42`
- **Physical effect:** Selects the family member and its expansion profile.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 0.8 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `seed_params.g_T`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.3 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `bounds.g_T`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `g_eps_m` — Labeled one-sided asymmetry (rows)

- **Summary:** Odd-cosine asymmetry amplitude; nonzero forces the seam-free full ring.
- **Detail:** LABELED RECONSTRUCTION/HYPOTHESIS: the verified Fourcade march plus an odd-cosine single-sided term. No published asymmetric-per-azimuth JMLC construction exists. The odd cosine breaks the left-right mirror, so a nonzero value forces the full model.
- **Formula:** `r *= 1 + eps_m w(s) cos(phi)`
- **Physical effect:** Widens one side and narrows the other.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 0.2 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `seed_params.g_eps_m`)
- **Search bounds:** -0.3 .. 0.3 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `bounds.g_eps_m`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=-0.3, hi=0.3, kind=—, section=— (`hornlab_search/design/generators/grid.py` `ROWS_MAX_EPS`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_lecleach_asym_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/JMLC_FAMILY_VARIANTS_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.5 Up/down-asymmetric saddle
- **Flags:** —

#### `g_eps_start` — Asymmetry window start (rows)

- **Summary:** Arc fraction where the one-sided asymmetry opens; the throat stays exact.
- **Detail:** The smoothstep window keeps the throat at the driver's exit radius and angle. This is part of the labeled reconstruction, not a published rule.
- **Formula:** `w(s) = smoothstep from eps_start to the mouth`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the one-sided widening toward or away from the mouth.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 0.0 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `seed_params.g_eps_start`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 0.5 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `bounds.g_eps_start`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_lecleach_asym_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/JMLC_FAMILY_VARIANTS_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.5 Up/down-asymmetric saddle
- **Flags:** —

#### `g_fc_hz` — Axisymmetric rows cutoff (rows) (Hz)

- **Summary:** Single cutoff of an axisymmetric Le Cleac'h rows march.
- **Detail:** The verified Fourcade march has no per-azimuth split, so fc_hz is the only cutoff. The iwata and AR stretch keep this axisymmetric base.
- **Formula:** `m = 4 pi fc / c`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the rollback size and loading corner.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 800.0 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `seed_params.g_fc_hz`)
- **Search bounds:** 600.0 .. 1200.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `bounds.g_fc_hz`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_lecleach_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/JMLC_LECLEACH_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ## 2. Construction comparison
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `g_stop_deg` — Rows march stop (rows) (deg)

- **Summary:** Wall-angle stop of the per-azimuth rows march; the legal window depends on the kind.
- **Detail:** The verified Le Cleac'h rows use the published stop window and allow rollback past vertical. The Salmon rows stop below or above the cap-area asymptote, and modulated Salmon rows keep the plan's rollback window. A per-typology note narrows this.
- **Formula:** `march stops when phi >= stop_deg`
- **Physical effect:** Chooses how far around the rollback each row is followed.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 170.0 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `seed_params.g_stop_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 120.0 .. 240.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `bounds.g_stop_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `n_phi` — Azimuth row count

- **Summary:** Number of azimuth rows in the rows/grid model.
- **Detail:** The row count sets the angular sampling of the point grid. The quarter model, half model and full ring each have their own minimum and parity rules in the generator. It is a count, not a length.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises angular fidelity at the cost of more grid rows.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 25 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `generator.n_phi`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_s` — Axial station count

- **Summary:** Number of axial stations along each row.
- **Detail:** Each row is sampled at equal axial (or equal-arc, for the rows march) fractions. This count sets the longitudinal resolution. It is a count, not a length.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises longitudinal fidelity at the cost of more points.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 32 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `generator.n_s`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `span` — Row span

- **Summary:** How much of the azimuth circle the rows model covers.
- **Detail:** The legal spans are the ones the generator handles, and their names are read from the generator's own span constant rather than retyped here. A kind can force a span: an odd-cosine asymmetry forces the full ring and a b-term forces the half model.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Chooses whether the mesh uses mirror planes or a seam-free full ring.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** full (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `generator.span`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=str, section=— (`hornlab_search/design/generators/grid.py` `ROWS_SPANS`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_span_requested`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/JMLC_FAMILY_VARIANTS_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.5 Up/down-asymmetric saddle
- **Flags:** —

#### `wall_thickness_mm` — Shell thickness (mm)

- **Summary:** Outer-shell thickness of a freestanding rows model.
- **Detail:** The point-grid route builds an explicit outer shell offset from each row's own profile. This is the printed shell thickness, not an acoustic dimension. The generator also uses it in its self-approach guard.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Changes the printed wall thickness, not the acoustic inner surface.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 3.0 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_asym_odd.yaml` `generator.wall_thickness_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

### lecleach_iwata_ar — FREEFORM / rows

- **Group:** organic
- **Symmetry:** hv
- **Config:** `examples/typologies/lecleach_iwata_ar.yaml`
- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/organic_rollback_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** Le Cleac'h exact meridian + area-preserving AR evolution (AR_m/AR_p, T=0.5 pinned), our reconstruction; 4 knobs

#### `g_T` — Rows family factor (rows)

- **Summary:** Salmon family T on the Salmon rows; Fourcade area-law T on the Le Cleac'h rows.
- **Detail:** The two row families share the spelling but not the equation. The Salmon rows use it in the family area law; the verified Le Cleac'h rows use it in the Fourcade wavefront-area law.
- **Formula:** `Salmon family law; Fourcade eq. 42`
- **Physical effect:** Selects the family member and its expansion profile.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 0.5 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_iwata_ar.yaml` `seed_params.g_T`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.5 .. 0.5 (search=false, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_iwata_ar.yaml` `bounds.g_T`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `g_ar_m` — Reconstructed mouth AR (rows)

- **Summary:** Mouth aspect ratio of our area-preserving stretch; a reconstruction, not the product.
- **Detail:** The base march is the verified Fourcade construction. The AR evolution is our reconstruction of the published principle and is explicitly not the Iwata product. T is pinned by this config.
- **Formula:** `AR evolution with ar_m at the mouth`
- **Physical effect:** Makes the mouth wider than tall (or the reverse).
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 2.0 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_iwata_ar.yaml` `seed_params.g_ar_m`)
- **Search bounds:** 1.2 .. 2.8 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_iwata_ar.yaml` `bounds.g_ar_m`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_iwata_ar_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/JMLC_FAMILY_VARIANTS_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.2 Iwata / quasi-Iwata / "FH-N2"
- **Flags:** —

#### `g_ar_p` — Reconstructed AR exponent (rows)

- **Summary:** Exponent of our area-preserving aspect-ratio stretch.
- **Detail:** This shapes where the reconstructed AR change happens. No published AR construction exists for this class; the label stays reconstruction.
- **Formula:** `AR(l) = 1 + (ar_m - 1) (l / L0)^ar_p`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the aspect-ratio change toward the throat or mouth.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 1.0 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_iwata_ar.yaml` `seed_params.g_ar_p`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.5 .. 2.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_iwata_ar.yaml` `bounds.g_ar_p`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_iwata_ar_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/JMLC_FAMILY_VARIANTS_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.2 Iwata / quasi-Iwata / "FH-N2"
- **Flags:** —

- **Default:** 600.0 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_toroidal.yaml` `seed_params.g_fc_hz`)
- **Search bounds:** 600.0 .. 1200.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_toroidal.yaml` `bounds.g_fc_hz`)

#### `g_r_lip_mm` — Inferred torus lip (rows) (mm)

- **Summary:** Tangent-continuous circular lip on the verified rollback; [inferred] analogue.
- **Detail:** No published torus-lip math exists for this class. The arc is tangent to the verified wall and curls back for half a turn. It is labeled [inferred], not a reproduction of a product.
- **Formula:** `circular arc tangent at the joint, wrap = half a turn`
- **Physical effect:** Adds a rolled-back lip at the mouth.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 30.0 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_toroidal.yaml` `seed_params.g_r_lip_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 6.0 .. 35.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_toroidal.yaml` `bounds.g_r_lip_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=100.0, kind=—, section=— (`hornlab_search/design/generators/grid.py` `ROWS_MAX_R_LIP_MM`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid.append_tangent_lip`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/JMLC_FAMILY_VARIANTS_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.3 Ferguson / toroidal roll

#### `g_stop_deg` — Toroidal rollback stop (rows) (deg)

- **Summary:** Stop of the verified Fourcade march before the inferred lip is appended.
- **Detail:** The base march remains the published Fourcade construction (R9-J). Only the lip continuation is inferred. The grid's stop window applies.
- **Formula:** `march stops when phi >= stop_deg`
- **Physical effect:** Sets how far the wall turns before the lip begins.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 170.0 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_toroidal.yaml` `seed_params.g_stop_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 120.0 .. 240.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_toroidal.yaml` `bounds.g_stop_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `n_phi` — Azimuth row count

- **Summary:** Number of azimuth rows in the rows/grid model.
- **Detail:** The row count sets the angular sampling of the point grid. The quarter model, half model and full ring each have their own minimum and parity rules in the generator. It is a count, not a length.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises angular fidelity at the cost of more grid rows.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 15 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_toroidal.yaml` `generator.n_phi`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_s` — Axial station count

- **Summary:** Number of axial stations along each row.
- **Detail:** Each row is sampled at equal axial (or equal-arc, for the rows march) fractions. This count sets the longitudinal resolution. It is a count, not a length.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises longitudinal fidelity at the cost of more points.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 32 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_toroidal.yaml` `generator.n_s`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `span` — Row span

- **Summary:** How much of the azimuth circle the rows model covers.
- **Detail:** The legal spans are the ones the generator handles, and their names are read from the generator's own span constant rather than retyped here. A kind can force a span: an odd-cosine asymmetry forces the full ring and a b-term forces the half model.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Chooses whether the mesh uses mirror planes or a seam-free full ring.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** — (source: —)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=str, section=— (`hornlab_search/design/generators/grid.py` `ROWS_SPANS`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_span_requested`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/JMLC_FAMILY_VARIANTS_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.5 Up/down-asymmetric saddle
- **Flags:** —

#### `wall_thickness_mm` — Shell thickness (mm)

- **Summary:** Outer-shell thickness of a freestanding rows model.
- **Detail:** The point-grid route builds an explicit outer shell offset from each row's own profile. This is the printed shell thickness, not an acoustic dimension. The generator also uses it in its self-approach guard.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Changes the printed wall thickness, not the acoustic inner surface.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 3.0 (source: `examples/typologies/lecleach_toroidal.yaml` `generator.wall_thickness_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)

### salmon_elliptical — FREEFORM / rows

- **Group:** organic
- **Symmetry:** hv
- **Config:** `examples/typologies/salmon_elliptical.yaml`
- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/organic_rollback_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** per-azimuth spherical_salmon rows (fc_h 800 / fc_v 1100 seed), rollback stop; 4 knobs; labeled hypothesis

#### `g_T` — Salmon family factor (rows)

- **Summary:** Hyperbolic-exponential family T of the per-azimuth Salmon rows.
- **Detail:** This is the Salmon family factor used inside the rows area law. The rows are a LABELED HYPOTHESIS-SHAPE EXPERIMENT, not a published construction.
- **Formula:** `Salmon family law; Fourcade eq. 42`
- **Physical effect:** Selects the Salmon family member used on every azimuth.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 0.8 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_elliptical.yaml` `seed_params.g_T`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.3 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/salmon_elliptical.yaml` `bounds.g_T`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `g_fc_h_hz` — Salmon H cutoff (rows) (Hz)

- **Summary:** Horizontal endpoint of the per-azimuth Salmon cutoff blend.
- **Detail:** Each row's cutoff is a cosine-squared blend of this value and the vertical one, giving the row family its H/V split. The pair must be supplied together.
- **Formula:** `1 / fc(phi) = cos^2(phi) / fc_h + sin^2(phi) / fc_v`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the horizontal rollback size.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 800.0 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_elliptical.yaml` `seed_params.g_fc_h_hz`)
- **Search bounds:** 600.0 .. 1200.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/salmon_elliptical.yaml` `bounds.g_fc_h_hz`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `g_fc_v_hz` — Vertical cutoff frequency (rows) (Hz)

- **Summary:** Vertical-plane Salmon cutoff in the per-azimuth cutoff blend.
- **Detail:** The vertical endpoint of the same cutoff blend. Supplying only one of the pair is refused, because the per-azimuth law would otherwise be undefined. It is the H/V split's vertical half.
- **Formula:** `1 / fc(phi) = cos^2(phi) / fc_h + sin^2(phi) / fc_v`
- **Physical effect:** Changes the vertical-plane rollback size and rate.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 1100.0 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_elliptical.yaml` `seed_params.g_fc_v_hz`)
- **Search bounds:** 600.0 .. 1200.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/salmon_elliptical.yaml` `bounds.g_fc_v_hz`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `g_stop_deg` — Salmon rows rollback stop (rows) (deg)

- **Summary:** Stop of the per-azimuth Salmon march; the cap-area law has an asymptote near half a turn.
- **Detail:** LABELED HYPOTHESIS-SHAPE EXPERIMENT (WP9b dossier section 2.4): per-azimuth Salmon marches, not an attributed published construction. The shipped seed stops below the asymptote and the plan's modulated window stays a rollback.
- **Formula:** `march stops when phi >= stop_deg`
- **Physical effect:** Follows the Salmon wall further or less far around the rollback.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 150.0 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_elliptical.yaml` `seed_params.g_stop_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 120.0 .. 240.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/salmon_elliptical.yaml` `bounds.g_stop_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)

- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)

### salmon_ripple12 — FREEFORM / rows

- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/organic_rollback_1in.yaml`

#### `g_T` — Rows family factor (rows)

- **Summary:** Salmon family T on the Salmon rows; Fourcade area-law T on the Le Cleac'h rows.
- **Detail:** The two row families share the spelling but not the equation. The Salmon rows use it in the family area law; the verified Le Cleac'h rows use it in the Fourcade wavefront-area law.
- **Formula:** `Salmon family law; Fourcade eq. 42`
- **Physical effect:** Selects the family member and its expansion profile.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 0.8 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_ripple12.yaml` `seed_params.g_T`)

- **Summary:** Horizontal-plane Salmon cutoff in the per-azimuth cutoff blend.

- **Formula:** `1 / fc(phi) = cos^2(phi) / fc_h + sin^2(phi) / fc_v`
- **Physical effect:** Changes the horizontal-plane rollback size and rate.

#### `g_fc_v_hz` — Vertical cutoff frequency (rows) (Hz)

- **Summary:** Vertical-plane Salmon cutoff in the per-azimuth cutoff blend.
- **Detail:** The vertical endpoint of the same cutoff blend. Supplying only one of the pair is refused, because the per-azimuth law would otherwise be undefined. It is the H/V split's vertical half.
- **Formula:** `1 / fc(phi) = cos^2(phi) / fc_h + sin^2(phi) / fc_v`

#### `g_ripple_amp_mm` — Pleats amplitude (rows) (mm)

- **Summary:** Peak radius deviation of the even-lobe mouth ripple, in millimetres.
- **Detail:** LABELED EXPERIMENT (the owner's 'pleats' feature): no published audio mouth-ripple construction exists. The amplitude is delivered at the widest station inside the opening window.
- **Formula:** `delta_r = amp w(s) cos(N phi)`
- **Physical effect:** Deepens or shallows the twelve pleats.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 3.0 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_ripple12.yaml` `seed_params.g_ripple_amp_mm`)

- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MOUTH_RIPPLE_SERRATION_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ## 6. Knob/scoring scheme for the campaign (proposal, ours)

#### `g_ripple_lobes` — Pleats lobe count (rows)

- **Summary:** Even lobe count of the mouth ripple; even cosines keep the left-right mirror.
- **Detail:** The shipped experiment uses an even count so the quarter/half mirror stays valid. Odd counts are refused by the generator. This is a labeled experiment.
- **Formula:** `r *= 1 + amp w(s) cos(N phi), N even`
- **Physical effect:** Sets the number of pleats around the mouth.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 12 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_ripple12.yaml` `seed_params.g_ripple_lobes`)

- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MOUTH_RIPPLE_SERRATION_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ## 4. Construction math we can implement (ours, the rows route)

### salmon_saddle — FREEFORM / rows

- **Group:** organic
- **Symmetry:** hv
- **Config:** `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml`
- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/organic_rollback_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** catalog
- **Description:** Salmon rows + explicit HALF span and a fixed C2 a2/b1 stop saddle (top != bottom), labeled hypothesis; 4 knobs

#### `g_T` — Rows family factor (rows)

- **Summary:** Salmon family T on the Salmon rows; Fourcade area-law T on the Le Cleac'h rows.
- **Detail:** The two row families share the spelling but not the equation. The Salmon rows use it in the family area law; the verified Le Cleac'h rows use it in the Fourcade wavefront-area law.
- **Formula:** `Salmon family law; Fourcade eq. 42`
- **Physical effect:** Selects the family member and its expansion profile.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 0.8 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `seed_params.g_T`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.3 .. 1.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `bounds.g_T`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `g_fc_h_hz` — Horizontal cutoff frequency (rows) (Hz)

- **Summary:** Horizontal-plane Salmon cutoff in the per-azimuth cutoff blend.
- **Detail:** Each azimuth's Salmon march gets its own cutoff from a cosine-squared blend of the horizontal and vertical values. This is the horizontal endpoint. The pair must always be supplied together.
- **Formula:** `1 / fc(phi) = cos^2(phi) / fc_h + sin^2(phi) / fc_v`
- **Physical effect:** Changes the horizontal-plane rollback size and rate.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 800.0 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `seed_params.g_fc_h_hz`)
- **Search bounds:** 600.0 .. 1200.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `bounds.g_fc_h_hz`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `g_fc_v_hz` — Vertical cutoff frequency (rows) (Hz)

- **Summary:** Vertical-plane Salmon cutoff in the per-azimuth cutoff blend.
- **Detail:** The vertical endpoint of the same cutoff blend. Supplying only one of the pair is refused, because the per-azimuth law would otherwise be undefined. It is the H/V split's vertical half.
- **Formula:** `1 / fc(phi) = cos^2(phi) / fc_h + sin^2(phi) / fc_v`
- **Physical effect:** Changes the vertical-plane rollback size and rate.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 1200.0 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `seed_params.g_fc_v_hz`)
- **Search bounds:** 600.0 .. 1200.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `bounds.g_fc_v_hz`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `g_stop_cos2k` — Saddle stop cosines (rows)

- **Summary:** Even-cosine stop terms that shape the saddle's cardinal/diagonal rollback.
- **Detail:** The LIST contract adds the cos-2k terms to the stop law. Even cosines are quarter-safe. The saddle is a LABELED HYPOTHESIS over the Salmon rows.
- **Formula:** `stop(phi) = stop0 (1 + sum a_2k cos(2k phi))`
- **Physical effect:** Deepens the rollback on the cardinal or diagonal planes.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** [0.25] (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `seed_params.g_stop_cos2k`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid.rows_task_harmonics`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `g_stop_deg` — Rows march stop (rows) (deg)

- **Summary:** Wall-angle stop of the per-azimuth rows march; the legal window depends on the kind.
- **Detail:** The verified Le Cleac'h rows use the published stop window and allow rollback past vertical. The Salmon rows stop below or above the cap-area asymptote, and modulated Salmon rows keep the plan's rollback window. A per-typology note narrows this.
- **Formula:** `march stops when phi >= stop_deg`
- **Physical effect:** Chooses how far around the rollback each row is followed.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 160.0 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `seed_params.g_stop_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 120.0 .. 240.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `bounds.g_stop_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `g_stop_sin2k1` — Saddle stop sines (rows)

- **Summary:** Odd-sine stop terms that make the saddle's top and bottom differ.
- **Detail:** The LIST contract adds the sin-(2k+1) terms. Their presence forces the half model. The saddle is a LABELED HYPOTHESIS over the Salmon rows.
- **Formula:** `stop(phi) = stop0 (1 + sum b_(2k+1) sin((2k+1) phi))`
- **Physical effect:** Makes the top and bottom rollback differ.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** [0.2] (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `seed_params.g_stop_sin2k1`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid.rows_task_harmonics`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_phi` — Azimuth row count

- **Summary:** Number of azimuth rows in the rows/grid model.
- **Detail:** The row count sets the angular sampling of the point grid. The quarter model, half model and full ring each have their own minimum and parity rules in the generator. It is a count, not a length.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises angular fidelity at the cost of more grid rows.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 25 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `generator.n_phi`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_s` — Axial station count

- **Summary:** Number of axial stations along each row.
- **Detail:** Each row is sampled at equal axial (or equal-arc, for the rows march) fractions. This count sets the longitudinal resolution. It is a count, not a length.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises longitudinal fidelity at the cost of more points.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 32 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `generator.n_s`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `span` — Row span

- **Summary:** How much of the azimuth circle the rows model covers.
- **Detail:** The legal spans are the ones the generator handles, and their names are read from the generator's own span constant rather than retyped here. A kind can force a span: an odd-cosine asymmetry forces the full ring and a b-term forces the half model.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Chooses whether the mesh uses mirror planes or a seam-free full ring.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** half (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `generator.span`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=str, section=— (`hornlab_search/design/generators/grid.py` `ROWS_SPANS`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_span_requested`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/JMLC_FAMILY_VARIANTS_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.5 Up/down-asymmetric saddle
- **Flags:** —

#### `wall_thickness_mm` — Shell thickness (mm)

- **Summary:** Outer-shell thickness of a freestanding rows model.
- **Detail:** The point-grid route builds an explicit outer shell offset from each row's own profile. This is the printed shell thickness, not an acoustic dimension. The generator also uses it in its self-approach guard.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Changes the printed wall thickness, not the acoustic inner surface.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 3.0 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_saddle.yaml` `generator.wall_thickness_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `g_x_amp` — Signed cardinal-in X amplitude (rows)

- **Summary:** Negative X amplitude opens the mouth along the cardinals (the signed reading).
- **Detail:** Signed-X v2 (owner-approved 2026-10-25): a negative value gives the cardinal-in / corner-out reading, while a positive value keeps the earlier diagonal-out reading. The magnitude cap is unchanged.
- **Formula:** `mouth modulation about the diagonal; sign selects the reading`
- **Physical effect:** Opens the mouth along the cardinals when negative.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** -0.15 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_x_signed.yaml` `seed_params.g_x_amp`)
- **Search bounds:** -0.4 .. 0.4 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/salmon_x_signed.yaml` `bounds.g_x_amp`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=-0.4, hi=0.4, kind=—, section=— (`hornlab_search/design/generators/grid.py` `ROWS_MAX_X_AMP`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/SALMON_X_HORN_EXPANSION_2026-10-25.md` § ### 3.1 X-shape v2 - signed, decoupled, and already half-implemented
- **Flags:** —

#### `g_x_fc` — X-shape depth fraction (rows)

- **Summary:** How far along the row the X-shape modulation grows.
- **Detail:** The X term is not applied at the throat; it grows toward the mouth. This fraction sets where that growth is strongest. It is dimensionless and is capped by the grid validator.
- **Formula:** `modulation weight grows with the arc fraction scaled by x_fc`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the X-shape's influence toward or away from the mouth.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 0.15 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_x_signed.yaml` `seed_params.g_x_fc`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 0.3 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/salmon_x_signed.yaml` `bounds.g_x_fc`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=0.3, kind=—, section=— (`hornlab_search/design/generators/grid.py` `ROWS_MAX_X_FC`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_shape_knobs`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/SALMON_X_HORN_EXPANSION_2026-10-25.md` § ### 3.1 X-shape v2 - signed, decoupled, and already half-implemented
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_phi` — Azimuth row count

- **Summary:** Number of azimuth rows in the rows/grid model.
- **Detail:** The row count sets the angular sampling of the point grid. The quarter model, half model and full ring each have their own minimum and parity rules in the generator. It is a count, not a length.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises angular fidelity at the cost of more grid rows.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 15 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_x_signed.yaml` `generator.n_phi`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_s` — Axial station count

- **Summary:** Number of axial stations along each row.
- **Detail:** Each row is sampled at equal axial (or equal-arc, for the rows march) fractions. This count sets the longitudinal resolution. It is a count, not a length.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises longitudinal fidelity at the cost of more points.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 32 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_x_signed.yaml` `generator.n_s`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

#### `span` — Row span

- **Summary:** How much of the azimuth circle the rows model covers.
- **Detail:** The legal spans are the ones the generator handles, and their names are read from the generator's own span constant rather than retyped here. A kind can force a span: an odd-cosine asymmetry forces the full ring and a b-term forces the half model.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Chooses whether the mesh uses mirror planes or a seam-free full ring.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** — (source: —)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=str, section=— (`hornlab_search/design/generators/grid.py` `ROWS_SPANS`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_span_requested`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/JMLC_FAMILY_VARIANTS_RESEARCH_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.5 Up/down-asymmetric saddle
- **Flags:** —

#### `wall_thickness_mm` — Shell thickness (mm)

- **Summary:** Outer-shell thickness of a freestanding rows model.
- **Detail:** The point-grid route builds an explicit outer shell offset from each row's own profile. This is the printed shell thickness, not an acoustic dimension. The generator also uses it in its self-approach guard.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Changes the printed wall thickness, not the acoustic inner surface.
- **Context:** `rows`
- **Default:** 3.0 (source: `examples/typologies/salmon_x_signed.yaml` `generator.wall_thickness_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.grid._rows_check_spec`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.4 organic/elliptical + X-shape (WP9b)
- **Flags:** —

### osse_axi_fusedcuda — FREEFORM / generator

- **Group:** uncatalogued
- **Symmetry:** unknown
- **Config:** `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml`
- **Brief:** `examples/briefs/axi_90x90_1in.yaml`
- **Source status:** uncatalogued
- **Description:** Config present in examples/typologies/ but not listed in examples/catalog.yaml.

#### `L_mm` — Depth (mm)

- **Summary:** Axial depth shared by both meridians, throat to mouth.
- **Detail:** The generator builds both meridians to one z span, so L_mm is shared rather than a per-plane length. It is the fixed-length dimension for conical, exponential, hypex and OSSE; length-derived types may only be shortened to it. The brief envelope still bounds the final depth.
- **Formula:** `z = s L, with s the normalised axial fraction`
- **Physical effect:** Moves the mouth plane in or out along the horn axis.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 110.0 (source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `seed_params.L_mm`)
- **Search bounds:** 80.0 .. 140.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `bounds.L_mm`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.ICW.L_mm`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ## 4. FREEFORM
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_a_deg` — H-plane OSSE coverage half-angle (deg)

- **Summary:** Far-field wall half-angle of the OSSE meridian; the throat angle is the driver exit.
- **Detail:** The OSSE wall starts at the brief's driver exit angle and approaches this coverage half-angle. It is a half-angle in the mesher's convention.
- **Formula:** `OSSE: a is the far-field wall half-angle`
- **Physical effect:** Widens or narrows the OSSE mouth for a fixed depth.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 45.0 (source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `seed_params.h_a_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** 32.0 .. 50.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `bounds.h_a_deg`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.a_deg`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.build_meridian`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `h_n` — H-plane OSSE termination exponent

- **Summary:** Superellipse termination exponent of the OSSE meridian, active while s is nonzero.
- **Detail:** It controls how sharply the OSSE wall transitions into the terminated cross-section. The mesher accepts it as a plain finite number and requires it positive while s is nonzero.
- **Formula:** `OSSE termination exponent; bi-radial: r(z) = r0 + tan(a) z + c z^n`
- **Physical effect:** Sharpens or softens the mouth roll of an OSSE termination.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 4.0 (source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `seed_params.h_n`)
- **Search bounds:** 2.0 .. 8.0 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `bounds.h_n`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.n`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry._osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `h_s` — H-plane termination amplitude

- **Summary:** OSSE superellipse termination amplitude; zero disables the termination.
- **Detail:** A nonzero s turns on the OSSE superellipse termination and makes n and q active. The mesher requires a positive n and q while s is nonzero. A zero value leaves the plain OS wall.
- **Formula:** `OSSE superellipse termination (pkg/profile_formulas.py)`
- **Physical effect:** Rolls the mouth toward a superellipse outline instead of a plain OS wall.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.3 (source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `seed_params.h_s`)
- **Search bounds:** 0.0 .. 0.6 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `bounds.h_s`)
- **Hard limits:** lo=—, hi=—, kind=float, section=profile (`hornlab_search/design/families.py` `FAMILY_KEYS.OSSE.s`)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry._osse`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 1b. OSSE parameters
- **Flags:** —

#### `max_wall_deg` — Generator wall-angle cap (deg)

- **Summary:** Upper bound applied to every selected anchor's wall angle.
- **Detail:** The materializer clamps anchor angles into this cap after selecting and perturbing them. The same block key also supplies the default stop for a spherical-salmon march.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Caps how vertical the wall may become.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 80.0 (source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `generator.max_wall_deg`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/WP9_WP9B_GROUNDING_2026-10-25.md` § ### 2.3 `spherical_salmon` (G4)
- **Flags:** overloaded

#### `mouth` — Mouth cross-section

- **Summary:** Mouth cross-section family: ellipse, superellipse or rounded rectangle.
- **Detail:** The generator emits two cross-section stations: a circular throat and the selected mouth shape. Superellipse and rounded-rectangle mouths read the mouth exponent and corner-radius knobs respectively.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Changes the mouth outline without changing the meridians.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** ellipse (source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `generator.mouth`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.registry.materialize`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4d. Cross-section stations (crossSections)
- **Flags:** —

#### `n_anchors` — Anchor count

- **Summary:** Number of [z, r, wall-angle] anchors selected per meridian.
- **Detail:** The generator reduces the dense march to this many anchors before the mesher sees it. Both endpoints are always kept and interior anchors are placed by equal arc length in the normalised z/angle plane, so curved regions get more anchors.
- **Formula:** not stated
- **Physical effect:** Raises mesh fidelity at the cost of more control points.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 16 (source: `examples/typologies/osse_axi_fusedcuda.yaml` `generator.n_anchors`)
- **Search bounds:** none (this is a block value, not a searched knob)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.select_anchors`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/typologies/MESHER_TYPOLOGIES.md` § ### 4b. Profile representation
- **Flags:** —

#### `h_u0` — H-plane perturbation strength

- **Summary:** Per-anchor log-increment perturbation applied to the anchor radii.
- **Detail:** Each interior anchor increment is scaled by exp(u) before the radii are rebuilt from the throat. The legacy form moves the mouth size too, while the shape mode rescales the increments to preserve the parent mouth radius. A zero knob leaves the parent anchors untouched.
- **Formula:** `new_r[i+1] = new_r[i] + d_i exp(clip(u_i, -u_max, u_max))`
- **Physical effect:** Redistributes wall expansion between anchors.
- **Context:** `generator`
- **Default:** 0.0 (source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_osse_hv_fusedcuda.yaml` `seed_params.h_u0`)
- **Search bounds:** -0.25 .. 0.25 (search=true, log=false; source: `examples/typologies/perturbed_osse_hv_fusedcuda.yaml` `bounds.h_u0`)
- **Hard limits:** none (not a mesher family key)
- **Code:** `hornlab_search.design.generators.profiles.perturb_increments`
- **Doc:** `docs/research/senior_pass_2026-10-24/04_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` § ## WP3. Shape-only perturbations and graded infeasibility
- **Flags:** —
